## Fixed accepted-reference graph boundary: controlled test

The user authorized testing connections inside versus outside each group in
the fixed fusion graph as a stopping signal. This test keeps outsiders out of
that graph. It does not repeat the earlier all-corpus graph expansion, change
query scores, or adopt a new hierarchy admission rule.

Evidence: `target/food-halves-v1/fixed-reference-conductance-v1/` in the retained
task worktree. Plan SHA256
`d812bbd362f2bca5609a64e22ffc88697b4a7a4bbf8d82a69882bf49ea59409b`
was frozen before admission outcomes, binding the method, source/test bytes,
baseline, graphs, memberships, vectors and the same 1,449 queries. Food still
has 137 accepted references and 15 fixed fusion groups; Arts has 162 references
and 25 groups. Other-root membership is not negative gold, and synthetic root
names/definitions are not semantic scope constraints.

### Frozen rule

For each group, order all accepted references of that root by their unchanged
mean meaning-kernel affinity to group members, excluding the entire reference's
own source note. Equal scores enter together. At every score-level prefix,
measure fusion weight crossing the split and total weighted degree inside it.
Conductance is crossing weight divided by the smaller of the inside and outside
degree volumes. Graph weights are never compared directly with query scores.

Choose the minimum-conductance prefix touching the target group, restricted to
at most half the graph's degree volume. Prefer the earlier prefix for numerical
ties. Require a local minimum in crossing weight divided by **own** volume:
the next unequal value must rise, and the preceding value cannot be smaller.
Looking beyond the half-volume limit with this own-side fraction avoids a
spurious valley caused only by switching the conductance denominator. A zero
crossing-weight cut is resolved; an unresolved minimum causes abstention, with
no automatic second choice. No trimming or semantic score constant is used.

Map a resolved cut to the midpoint of its reference score and the next lower
score. Each outsider keeps the same mean group score and passes at or above
this threshold. The graph is accepted-reference-only and has no outsider
inputs. A graph prefix need not retain all group members; this does not delete
or redefine accepted references. Whole-note member recovery is measured
separately, with the same source note removed from both graph and means in each
fold. The primary remains ungated; hard all-fold gating is a diagnostic.

This is an experimental conductance sweep with a meaning-score ordering, not
a spectral ordering or a claimed graph-clustering guarantee. Graph degree
volume weights entries and their connections; it is not a distinct-note count.

### Actual outcomes

Compare against the previous neighbor-context, member-anchored `y-x` score bend
with the original lower-tail trimming. These are the same saved provisional
panels, not independent semantic accuracy estimates.

| Root, ungated | Plausible before → graph rule | Rejection controls before → graph rule | Proposed omitted notes before → graph rule |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Food | 21/30 → 12/30 | 3/51 → 1/51 | 43 → 18 |
| Arts | 17/24 → 5/24 | 3/20 → 0/20 | 174 → 21 |

The reduction in unwanted selections does not establish improvement: useful
selections fall substantially. Food loses ten previously proposed fits and
gains Steaks, net minus nine. Lost routes include Coffee, Tea, Coffee roasting,
Taste profiles, Coffee-drinking experience, the human-accepted pisanki Eggs,
Ice cream bars, Wineries and Mugs. Arts loses twelve earlier fits, including
Visual artworks, Cartoons, several Photography/Photographs entries, Art
commissions and Films; it gains no saved fit.

Food's one remaining provisional mismatch is Wildlife. Gas facilities is no
longer proposed into either root, and Nausea, Blood and JAIA Drake remain
excluded from Food. The three human-accepted Food notes now have only two
available routes: the pisanki note is lost entirely, not merely through its
low-priority Traditional activities heading. The rejected Easter-broadcast
note remains excluded. Arts labels remain provisional agent assessments;
Food decisions do not transfer into Arts automatically.

Ten Food groups and 21 Arts groups have a base boundary. The remaining five
and four respectively have no resolved minimum. Only one Food model and five
Arts models pass every whole-note fold. Hard gating proposes 0/30 Food fits
and 2/24 Arts fits, with zero saved rejections in either; this is abstention,
not a useful recovery policy.

### What the graph is selecting

Food preservation `fused-008` selects four closely related preservation entries,
with conductance `0.210932`. It leaves out the fifth, generic century-egg Food
reference, whose cross-note score is `0.047969`. The new threshold is
`0.113467`, versus the former `0.022297`. Gas facilities scores `0.047740` and
is excluded, but Eggs `0.055093`, Coffee roasting `0.028996`, Ice cream bars
`0.025003`, Wineries `0.028644` and Mugs `0.028942` lose this earlier route too.
The cut identifies a tightly connected preservation neighborhood; that does
not establish the full range of useful Food admissions.

General Food `fused-014`, an earlier Coffee/Tea route, has its smallest eligible
conductance at 71 references. The own-side crossing fraction continues falling
after half volume, so the candidate fails the predeclared local-valley test.
Arts Audio technology `fused-021` behaves similarly at 91 references. Its lack
of a boundary removes Gas/Earthquake routes but also many visual-art and film
routes. This is an explicit structural abstention, not evidence that those
queries have no semantic support.

A subsequent **hindsight capacity diagnostic**, not an automatic policy or a
second predictive experiment, examines every eligible local valley in these
saved curves. Allowing an oracle to choose separate valleys against the saved
rejection controls can recover at most 28/30 Food fits and 14/24 Arts fits while
excluding all controls. No chosen threshold vector is deployed. Thus the
minimum-selection rule leaves headroom, but choosing different local valleys
alone still cannot recover all saved Arts fits under these constraints. The
diagnostic does not resolve scope or qualify a cutoff learned from these labels.

**Conclusion:** this graph-boundary rule is not an improvement and is not
adopted. It finds tight accepted neighborhoods or abstains, without reliably
identifying the wider range of useful outside entries. Fixed graph evidence
must not be mistaken for a semantic membership certificate.

### Implementation, checks and retained evidence

`fixed_reference_graph.py` supplies the isolated experimental boundary and
whole-note recovery calculation. It imports the existing `mean_support` so
the query score cannot silently diverge. Six tests first fail for the absent
module, then pass through these calculation functions. They cover topology
changing the boundary while scores stay fixed, rejection of a false half-volume
valley in a uniform graph, whole-note removal, tied-score/permutation/scaling
behavior, unchanged inputs, isolated references and invalid inputs. All 63
combined graph/bend/query/fusion/HDBSCAN/local-expansion tests pass.

Independent dense-matrix sums verify 38,153 cut/volume rows across 255 base and
whole-note sweeps. Maximum cut error is `2.31e-14`, volume error `9.95e-14`.
All 57,960 query/group scores exactly match the earlier experiment. Query
reordering, duplicates, isolated evaluation, uniform graph scaling, unchanged
source/graph hashes and unchanged original hierarchy state pass. Evaluation
takes 3.65 seconds; no LLM calls, encodings, training or hierarchy/UI changes.

Artifacts retain the frozen plan and calculation script, full reference
curves/folds, all query/group and root predictions, changed proposals, note
controls, cohort metrics and verification. The two explicitly labeled
`*-valley-capacity-diagnostic.json` files contain the subsequent capacity
calculation, not validated admission thresholds. Reusable source, tests and
maintained findings land on local `main`; retain the task worktree because
these ignored source-bound evidence paths are needed for continued research.

## Weakest cross-note member as the boundary: controlled test

The user approved testing each fixed group's weakest existing member score
directly as its boundary, with the member's whole source note excluded when
scoring it. This removes reference-curve fitting and graph-cut selection.
It does not change fusion groups, weights, query scores or hierarchy admission.

Evidence: `target/food-halves-v1/weakest-member-boundary-v1/` in the retained
task worktree. Plan SHA256
`9f01b0f8e433b73ffff8d0d7814fc7697b2304a2d34eb7318dfc9b5f335c3746`
was frozen before outcomes. It binds the exact method/source/tests and prior
baselines, the same 15 Food and 25 Arts groups, 1,449 independent queries,
saved vectors, provisional cohorts and separate human controls. No LLM calls,
new encodings, training or paid resources are used.

For every member, compute the existing mean meaning-kernel affinity against
members of the same group from other notes. Take the exact smallest mean as
the boundary: no bend, trimming, interpolation with an adjacent reference,
numerical semantic margin or labeled threshold selection. An outsider passes
when its unchanged mean score reaches that boundary in any group. Other
accepted reference groups and outsiders cannot affect the boundary. A group
with no remaining members or no cross-note support supplies no boundary.
Fixed reference memberships are never expanded or redefined during scoring.

### Actual primary results

All counts below use the same saved provisional panels and ungated any-group
admission. They are development comparisons, not independent accuracy estimates.

| Root | Original score bend: fits / mismatches | Graph boundary: fits / mismatches | Weakest member: fits / mismatches |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Food | 21/30; 3/51 | 12/30; 1/51 | 14/30; 3/51 |
| Arts | 17/24; 3/20 | 5/24; 0/20 | 15/24; 0/20 |

The original score bend is the previous neighbor-context, member-anchored
`y-x` rule with the original lower-tail trimming. The graph boundary is the
preceding fixed-reference conductance experiment. Weakest-member Food proposes
40 omitted entries from 30 notes; Arts proposes 85 from 64 notes. These remain
proposals, not additions or validated coverage gains. All 40 groups have base
boundaries, so this experiment's losses are not caused by unavailable base
curves or cuts.

Compared with the original score bend, Food loses Coffee L00228, Tea L00534,
Coffee roasting L00616, Taste profiles L00617, Coffee-drinking experience
L00682, Ice cream bars L03892, Wineries L04122 and Mugs L05317. It gains Charity
dinners L02693. Tea L00267 still passes. The other eight missed provisional
fits were also missed by that baseline; do not count them as eight new losses.

Food excludes the two Wildlife entries L04627/L05566 and Gas facilities L06055
that were its earlier three provisional mismatches. It replaces them with
Visits to Japan L02274, Invitations L03286 and Travel classes L05800. The
unchanged total of three therefore conceals different proposed errors.

Arts removes all three earlier provisional mismatches: Earthquakes L01799,
Breath L04399 and Gas facilities L06055. Relative to the score-bend baseline,
it loses Cartoons L03388, Photography L03433 and Photographs L04802, and gains
Art galleries L03735. Its other six missed fits were already missed. Compared
with the graph boundary, it recovers ten fits without adding a saved mismatch.
This is a useful Arts development tradeoff, not qualification on unseen data.

All four prior human Food **note-level** controls match again: the three
accepted notes have a route and the Easter-broadcast note remains excluded.
In particular, the accepted pisanki note returns through Eggs L02984, while
its low-priority Traditional activities heading is still missed. Nausea
L02276, Blood L06803 and JAIA Drake L04019 remain excluded from Food. Those
three are entry-level human rejections, distinct from the note-level controls.
Arts labels remain provisional; none inherits a Food rejection automatically.

### Concrete boundary mechanisms

Food preservation `fused-008` now uses its weakest member, generic century-egg
Food L06359, at `0.0479689997`. Eggs L02984 scores `0.0550930106` and passes;
Gas facilities scores `0.0477396846` and fails. This explains the recovered
human-accepted note and the removed Gas route. It does not establish a robust
margin: the Gas separation is only about `0.0002293` under the saved vectors.

General Food `fused-014` has two members, Food L00445 and Food L06842. Their
mutual cross-note affinity is `0.1149465543`, which becomes the new threshold.
Coffee L00228 scores `0.084107` and now fails this route, formerly available
at the score-bend threshold `0.071951`. A group's internal similarity is
therefore not automatically the weakest useful outside membership score.

All three new Food mismatches enter through restaurant-related `fused-004`.
Its weakest member is Dining groups L05006:

> Dining groups are parties of two or more people dining together, which may
> include children.

Its score against other-note group members is `0.0288968293`, below the next
member Restaurant atmosphere L01119 at `0.0439631403`. The weakest-member rule
sets the cutoff at the former. Visits to Japan scores `0.0393471443`,
Invitations `0.0367863784`, Travel classes `0.0345618654`, and the gained fit
Charity dinners `0.0373254006`. All pass. Visits to Japan scoring above Charity
dinners means merely raising this group's threshold cannot retain that fit
through this group while excluding every mismatch. This is a local score-order
limitation, not evidence that the underlying Dining groups member is invalid
or that the global score is incapable of recovering Charity dinners elsewhere.
The complete member table and query definitions are retained in
`restaurant-group-trace.json`; no reference or judgment is changed.

Arts Audio technology `fused-021` uses Trying soundpacks L05675 at
`0.0399633316`, excluding Gas facilities `0.0258617412`. Arts Artists
`fused-010` uses Artist pages L00822 at `0.0372062174`, giving Art galleries
an available route. The remaining films, creator and visual-art misses are not
silently relabeled as unsuitable.

### Self-consistency and verification

Recovering the full group's existing members with the full-data minimum is
true by construction, not a validation result. Recompute the boundary in
whole-source-note exclusion folds to inspect sensitivity separately. Of 215
folds, 18 have no cross-note boundary after exclusion. One Food group and no
Arts group pass every fold. The optional hard gate yields 0 saved fits for
both roots; Food still proposes two unreviewed omitted entries, Arts none.
Do not adopt that gate or report abstention as improved accuracy.

Five tests first fail for the missing implementation, then pass through
`fixed_reference_members.derive_boundary`. They cover the exact minimum,
exclusion of every entry from a member's note, recomputation after note
removal, tied minima, unavailable support, nonmember/input invariance and
invalid inputs. All 68 combined member/graph/bend/query/fusion/HDBSCAN/local
expansion tests pass.

Independent scalar arithmetic checks all 255 base/fold boundaries and 1,898
member scores, with maximum error `2.22e-16`. All 57,960 outsider/group scores
exactly match the earlier baseline. Query order, duplicates and isolated
evaluation are invariant. Removing every nonmember reference leaves all 40
base boundaries exactly unchanged. Protected source/graph hashes and original
hierarchy state remain unchanged. Evaluation takes 1.60 seconds.

The new rule is **not adopted**: it is substantially better than the graph
boundary on Arts, but does not improve Food overall against the original score
bend. Retain it as an experimental comparison, with the concentrated restaurant
failure and overly strict Coffee route as separate evidence. Reusable source,
tests and maintained findings land on local `main`; the worktree remains for
the ignored frozen calculations, full member/fold results and all proposals.

## Auditing Arts selections outside the earlier controls

The user approved reviewing the weakest-member rule's Arts selections beyond
the existing controls. The rule, scores, fusion groups, meanings and hierarchy
remain unchanged. This is a complete **post-selection agent audit**, not new
human gold, an independently sampled test, or an admission-policy change.

Evidence: `target/food-halves-v1/arts-unreviewed-admissions-audit-v1/` in the
retained task worktree. The audit plan was frozen before reading the new fixed
meanings and full posts; selected titles and aggregate counts were already
observed. Plan SHA256
`1ea0bcd68e75c4b682ff96ea13b7b82830edadbbf59d7c49bcbf086824a0f271`.
Assessments were saved before joining winning groups, scores and thresholds;
assessment SHA256
`c3818ead142b7130df94501c2e9f9940601ab4aeb64e8e226497d97ba3497d5d`.
This masks numerical routes during review, not the known fact of selection.

### Scope and review contract

The saved rule proposes 86 query entries from 65 notes in total; 85 entries
from 64 notes are in the fixed entirely-omitted query cohort. Exclude **all 51**
earlier Arts label IDs: 24 provisional fits, 20 provisional mismatches and seven
unresolved entries. Sixteen of those IDs are selected: fifteen fits and one
unresolved AI case. Review every other selection, without sampling: **70
entries from 57 notes**, including 69 omitted-cohort entries from 56 notes.

Only 45 of those 57 note IDs were absent from the earlier Arts controls.
Fourteen reviewed entries share one of twelve earlier control notes. All 57
source texts are distinct. Neither new entry IDs nor source-note IDs establish
statistical independence; no independent accuracy rate follows from this audit.

Review each fixed meaning and its full source-post text. Do not redefine a
general meaning as only its post's application, treat another root's membership
as negative evidence, or impose the synthetic root definition as a fixed scope.
Use the actual accepted references, including their audiovisual, music, artist,
noise and communicative-silence meanings, as the present root context.

Mark clear expressive/media/sound/communication roles plausible, absent or
merely incidental roles unsupported, and genuine scope questions unresolved.
These are all agent assessments. Generic AI remains unresolved, consistent
with the earlier record. General hardware, platforms and broad meanings with
one media-specific source example are not forced into rejection. Classify
source-post relevance separately, so one suspect heading does not falsely
reject a note with another good route. Linked images/videos were not fetched;
missing attachment evidence remains a limitation where relevant.

### Complete audit results

| Assessment | Entries | Separate full-post assessment, distinct notes |
|---|---:|---:|
| Plausible | 39 | 40 |
| Unsupported / suspect | 10 | 6 |
| Unresolved | 21 | 11 |
| Total | 70 | 57 |

Entry and post columns answer different questions and are not interchangeable
precision estimates. For example, generic Servers is unresolved but selected
Graphics gives that note a direct visual-art route. Halls of fame is unresolved
as an any-field meaning while its particular source concerns video games.
Ownership and Output are suspect headings, but their source also has unresolved
general AI. Motherboards is unresolved as a hardware-scope question even though
its diagnostic-LED source post has no evident creative/media purpose.

Plausible selections include Pianos, Voice studios, DJs, video games, Anime,
Graphics, image galleries, picture frames, actors, creative works, publishing,
memes, botanical imagery and character designs. Flags and Questions have direct
symbolic/communicative meanings; they are not approved simply because they
occur in a social-media post. The Food-rejected Easter-broadcast post is
plausible here through Broadcasts; that earlier Food decision does not transfer.

The earlier **0/20 Arts mismatches** does not establish success on this wider
selection. The new audit identifies these ten suspect entry routes:

| Entry | Fixed topic / source evidence | Passing group | Score | Boundary |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|
| L00538 · Wondering | Internal consideration; wondering about heavy tea consumption | Noise/silence | 0.045567 | 0.043530 |
| L02575 · GraphQL | API query language/runtime; a software commit | Audio technology | 0.050402 | 0.039963 |
| L03821 · Science | Natural-world investigation; lunar research instruments | Audio technology | 0.046838 | 0.039963 |
| L03969 · Vector arithmetic | Vector operations; a triangle-centroid proof | Audio technology | 0.068287 | 0.039963 |
| L03970 · Geometric parameterizations | Mathematical representations; the same centroid proof | Audio technology | 0.042549 | 0.039963 |
| L04026 · Ownership | General property/control rights; AI economic incentives | Audio technology | 0.041222 | 0.039963 |
| L04028 · Output | Any process result; the same AI economic source | Audio technology | 0.040333 | 0.039963 |
| L04079 · Indifference | Little interest/concern; reactions to Tesla models | Noise/silence | 0.071974 | 0.043530 |
| L04228 · Analysis | Generic detailed examination; short text and an unidentified linked video | Audio technology | 0.047112 | 0.039963 |
| L04878 · Spaces in configuration text | Whitespace introduced by a software parser | Audio technology | 0.067710 | 0.039963 |

These are provisional suspected mismatches, not ten newly human-confirmed
errors. In particular, interpreting every machine request as communication
would be a broader scope decision, not an established reason to accept GraphQL.
A general Science heading is not automatically justified by the one accepted
biological-signaling reference. The full meanings, source texts and rationales
for all 70 entries are in the compact `review.md` and structured evidence.

### Concentration in two routes

All ten suspects pass **only one** group each: eight through Audio technology
`fused-021`, two through Noise/silence `fused-024`. Within this audit, Audio
technology also proposes ten plausible and fourteen unresolved entries;
Noise/silence proposes three plausible and one unresolved. Counts across
groups overlap for entries with several routes and must not be summed into
unique-entry totals. None of the other passing groups proposes one of these
ten suspects; that is bounded audit evidence, not a semantic certificate.

Audio technology's minimum remains Trying soundpacks L05675 at
`0.0399633316`. Noise/silence's minimum is the figurative biological Signaling
silence L05169 at `0.0435299290`. These are legitimate fixed members, not
authorized deletions. The scores do not establish which individual words or
encoder features caused a match; no word ablation was performed.

Raising just those boundaries does not preserve current useful routes:
Vector arithmetic `0.068287` and configuration spaces `0.067710` both exceed
Gaming L01596 `0.051793` and Video gaming L06169 `0.040622`, whose current
only passing group is Audio technology. Indifference `0.071974` exceeds
Refusal to discuss life L02608 `0.064919` and Questions L04522 `0.049711`,
whose current only route is Noise/silence. Shouting scores `0.083057` and would
survive that particular increase. Dropping the groups wholesale is neither
proposed nor adopted. Alternative boundaries in other groups could change
available routes, so these local inversions alone are not a global impossibility
claim.

### Conditional limit of all group cutoffs

A subsequent arithmetic capacity diagnostic combines the old controls with
the new frozen assessments: **63 plausible and 30 unsupported entries**.
Exclude unresolved cases. The present rule selects 54/63 plausible entries
and 10/30 unsupported entries. To reject every unsupported entry under the
existing any-group rule, each group cutoff must exceed that group's largest
unsupported score. A plausible entry remains recoverable only if it scores
above that largest unsupported score in at least one group.

Under these provisional judgments, arbitrary separate cutoffs can recover at
most **57/63** plausible entries while rejecting all thirty controls. Six are
blocked in every group:

- Avatar: The Last Airbender films L03389.
- The Lord of the Rings film trilogy L03393.
- JAIA Drake L04019.
- Cartoons L05979.
- Refusal to discuss life L02608.
- Questions L04522.

For each of these, every one of the 25 groups has at least one unsupported
score as high or higher; the blocking example can differ by group. The complete
1,575 positive/group calculations, all blocking identities/scores, and all
150 blocked-entry/group witnesses are saved. No threshold vector is fitted or
deployed. This conditional capacity bound is not accuracy or an automatic
selection result. It depends on the agent judgments and does not turn them into
human gold. A better boundary may still improve the current tradeoff; the
calculation rules out a **perfect cutoff-only solution to these judgments**, not
all further boundary work.

### Verification and disposition

The complete selected-minus-old-label set matches the audit exactly. Assessment
IDs are unique, all 57 note-level assessments are consistent, all passing
routes and exact member-minimum boundaries match saved evidence, and independent
scalar comparisons replay the expanded capacity result. Assessment bytes remain
unchanged after revealing numerical routes. Protected input hashes, saved
results, meanings, groups, graphs and the original hierarchy state remain
unchanged. This documentation/evidence task changes no calculation behavior and
requires no new production API or test seam. All verification assertions pass.

The weakest-member rule remains **experimental, not adopted**. The audit finds
additional useful selections and exposes previously unseen suspect routes.
It makes zero model calls, encodings, clustering runs or training runs, and
changes no hierarchy, prompt, UI or policy. Keep generic AI and hardware scope
questions unresolved rather than quietly expanding the negative controls.
Reusable findings land on local `main`; retain the worktree for the frozen
review packet, assessments, source posts, score routes and capacity evidence.

## Saved title vectors on the expanded Arts cases

The user approved checking whether saved title vectors distinguish the newly
audited mismatches from useful Arts entries. This is a representation/ranking
probe, not a new admission rule. The 25 accepted fusion groups, 162 reference
entries, all meanings, graphs and source-note rules remain fixed. Outsiders
are evaluated independently and never enter the reference graph. No labels
change and no LLM, encoding, training or clustering runs occur.

Evidence: `target/food-halves-v1/arts-title-ordering-v1/` in the retained task
worktree. Plan SHA256
`37c5504cc01233f49308593afba2c10250e925ea6dc54429a336becca9548101`
was frozen before title-score outcomes. It binds both source vector stores and
their receipts/context mappings, existing scores, the earlier audit's immutable
assessments, all 1,449 queries and the exact numerical calculation.

### Single controlled change

Keep the same score: mean `exp[-5.5 * (1 - cosine)]` over the group's members,
excluding every reference from the query's source note. Compare meaning-to-
meaning affinities with title-to-title affinities. Neither method averages
over all outsiders or changes any group. The title vector is **not** a
context-free subject vector. Both saved representations encode:

```
{title}

{full fixed meaning}
```

Title pooling selects the title tokens; meaning pooling selects the full
meaning tokens. The other text remains context. Stored receipt offsets are
`3 * input_index + 0` for title and `+ 1` for meaning; no re-encoding is needed.
The two stores use the same frozen model/checkpoint and exact matching text.

The primary includes eight previously documented wrong within-group rankings
and conditional score separability on the entire expanded panel: 63 provisional
fits and 30 provisional mismatches. The new audit is selected using an earlier
meaning-based rule; it is not an unbiased independent comparison population.
Earlier unresolved cases and the audit's 21 unresolved entries remain unlabeled.
All query scores are saved, without silently treating those cases as negatives.

No meaning cutoff is reused on title scores. No fixed cutoff, blended score,
fitted weights, selected threshold vector or new admissions are tested here.

### Targeted mistakes: three fixes out of eight

The eight pairs were frozen before the title scores, not selected from favorable
title results. All are wrong under the existing mean-meaning group score.

| Fixed comparison group | Useful / unwanted | Meaning: useful / unwanted | Title: useful / unwanted | Title order correct? |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| Audio technology | Gaming / Vector arithmetic | 0.051793 / 0.068287 | 0.007411 / 0.015208 | No |
| Audio technology | Video gaming / Vector arithmetic | 0.040622 / 0.068287 | 0.008549 / 0.015208 | No |
| Audio technology | Gaming / Configuration spaces | 0.051793 / 0.067710 | 0.007411 / 0.014199 | No |
| Audio technology | Video gaming / Configuration spaces | 0.040622 / 0.067710 | 0.008549 / 0.014199 | No |
| Noise/silence | Refusal to discuss life / Indifference | 0.064919 / 0.071974 | 0.062346 / 0.027738 | Yes |
| Noise/silence | Questions / Indifference | 0.049711 / 0.071974 | 0.016955 / 0.027738 | No |
| Audio technology | Pianos / Science | 0.043437 / 0.046838 | 0.021325 / 0.009332 | Yes |
| Audio technology | Voice studios / GraphQL | 0.049582 / 0.050402 | 0.106765 / 0.006453 | Yes |

Thus titles supply useful distinctions for three cases, but the four gaming

### Broader separation gets worse

Conditional capacity asks how many plausible entries could pass **any separate
group cutoffs** while all supplied mismatch controls fail. It is a mathematical
upper bound using provisional judgments, not an automatic selection result or
a trained/deployed cutoff vector. Largest-raw-group pair orderings are a
secondary diagnostic; they are dependent comparisons, not admission accuracy.

| Panel / measurement | Meanings | Titles |
|---|---:|---:|
| Expanded: conditional cutoff capacity | 57/63 | 48/63 |
| Old controls only: conditional capacity | 24/24 | 22/24 |
| New audit only: conditional capacity | 37/39 | 27/39 |
| Expanded: useful above unwanted by largest group score | 1599/1890 | 1426/1890 |
| Old controls only: useful above unwanted | 434/480 | 428/480 |
| New audit only: useful above unwanted | 272/390 | 239/390 |

Panels overlap and must not be pooled as independent samples. Switching to
titles fixes 159 expanded largest-score orderings but worsens 332; 1,399 are
unchanged. On the old panel the corresponding change is 23 fixes versus 29
regressions; on the new audit it is 53 fixes versus 86 regressions.

Titles make three previously blocked cases conditionally recoverable: JAIA
Drake L04019, Cartoons L05979 and Refusal to discuss life L02608. They newly
block twelve: National flags L01660; five Internet memes entries L01810,
L01911, L02964, L04130 and L06342; Narrative endings L02965; Sumika L03877;
Netflix L04646; Color palettes L05679; Botanical imagery L06053; and Academy
Awards L06413. Avatar films L03389, The Lord of the Rings trilogy L03393 and
Questions L04522 remain blocked under either representation. None is relabeled
as unsuitable to make the representation look better.

**Do not replace mean-meaning scores with mean-title scores.** The title view
has complementary information in a few cases but is weaker on this expanded
panel. This does not prove a combined score is better: the earlier Food/Tools
combination experiment was mixed, and no combination is tested in this probe.
The eight pair results also show why a title-only condition is not a general
repair for the technical/mathematical Audio matches.

Existing `mean_support` and `separation_bounds` perform the calculation; no new

match. The normalized meaning vectors exactly match the previously saved
meaning-vector matrix, with maximum error zero.

All 36,225 meaning query/group scores replay exactly, as does the preceding

`1.06e-15`; scalar averaging checks 4,650 labeled group means, maximum error
`2.78e-17`. Query order, duplicates and isolated evaluation are invariant.
Protected sources, labels, accepted graphs and original hierarchy state remain
unchanged. All verification assertions pass.

Only query-to-accepted-reference kernels are built: 1,449 by 162 per view.
There is no outsider/outside kernel or graph. Read each raw store once, use four

## Existing combined query score on the expanded Arts cases

The user approved testing the existing title-and-meaning calculation on the
same expanded Arts cases. Keep all 25 fusion groups, 162 accepted references,
1,449 independent query entries, saved vectors, meanings and provisional
assessments fixed. No outsider enters a graph and no graph is recomputed.
This tests query scoring only, not a new admission or ending rule.

Evidence: `target/food-halves-v1/arts-combined-query-v1/` in the retained task
worktree. Plan SHA256
`b64ffffa425fda14266733f331c66e920888f9b71af14eb2d3ae778258e69fe1`
was frozen before the combined scores. Reuse the preceding exact title/meaning
query-to-reference kernel matrices rather than re-encode or recompute them.

### Existing calculation and matched controls

Run the unchanged `fixed_reference_query.query_support` calculation:

```
sqrt(sum(C_ij * t_i * m_j) / sum(C_ij))
```

Here `t_i` and `m_j` are the query's title and meaning kernel affinities to
accepted entries `i` and `j`; `C_ij` is their saved within-group fusion weight.
Exclude self-connections and every connection between entries from the same
source note. The frozen query notes are disjoint from reference notes; that
condition is explicitly verified rather than assumed for other populations.
All 25 groups have surviving cross-note edge weight, so all methods share the
same groups with no fabricated unavailable scores.

Matched controls substitute meaning twice or title twice in that same equation.
Retain both original unweighted means to distinguish graph reweighting from
combining the two views. The previously tested simpler combination is also a
control: `sqrt(mean(t_i * m_i))`, using the same reference entry for both views
without connection weights. This removes only the extra graph use in query
scoring; it does not remove fusion from the original group construction.

No mixing coefficient, cutoff, labels, threshold vector or prospective
admissions are fitted. Conditional separability and largest-group score
orderings use the same provisional cohorts. Unresolved cases remain unlabeled.

### Results: small aggregate gain, no targeted breakthrough

Conditional capacity is the maximum plausible entries recoverable by arbitrary
separate group cutoffs while rejecting all supplied mismatches. It is a
hindsight score bound, not actual admissions. Pair counts are dependent raw
ranking comparisons, not accuracy. The expanded panel has 63 provisional fits
and 30 provisional mismatches.

| Calculation | Targeted pairs correct /8 | Expanded conditional capacity /63 | Useful above unwanted /1890 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Original mean meaning | 0 | 57 | 1599 |
| Original mean title | 3 | 48 | 1426 |
| Meaning alone through connections | 1 | 56 | 1604 |
| Title alone through connections | 3 | 47 | 1426 |
| Title + meaning through connections | 3 | 58 | 1623 |
| Title + meaning at the same reference, no connections | 3 | 58 | 1622 |

Combining the views through connections fixes Pianos/Science,
Voice studios/GraphQL and Refusal to discuss life/Indifference. Meaning alone
through connections already fixes Pianos/Science, so that particular gain
cannot be attributed solely to adding titles. Both combined versions leave
the four Gaming/Video gaming versus Vector arithmetic/Configuration spaces
inversions and Questions/Indifference wrong.

In Audio technology, the main combined score is `0.019761` for Gaming,
`0.018767` for Video gaming, `0.031174` for Vector arithmetic and `0.031887`
for configuration spaces. Its Noise/silence score is `0.028678` for Questions
versus `0.044817` for Indifference. The remaining incorrect orderings are
therefore not missing-cutoff artifacts.

The connection-based combination fixes 113 expanded largest-score orderings
and worsens 94 against the matched meaning-through-connections control. Against
the original unweighted mean meaning it fixes 113 and worsens 89. Against
the simpler combination it fixes **nine and worsens eight**. The latter two
methods have the same conditional capacity and the same targeted fixes, so
there is little evidence on this panel that extra query graph weighting earns
its complexity. This is a bounded observation, not a proof that connections
cannot help another root or a decision to remove the reference fusion stage.

These focal scores are proxies for admission into the broader root, not
containment judgments about the particular reference group. A plausible Gaming
entry does not have to be a kind of Audio technology; there is no dedicated
gaming group in the fixed accepted references. Its current single passing
Audio route explains why the local inversion matters, but does not justify
forcing that route to succeed. The full any-group diagnostic remains necessary.

Both combined methods leave exactly five entries blocked under the expanded
all-controls condition: Avatar films L03389, The Lord of the Rings trilogy
L03393, JAIA Drake L04019, Questions L04522 and Academy Awards L06413.
Relative to original mean meaning, they recover conditional routes for Cartoons
L05979 and Refusal to discuss life L02608 but newly block Academy Awards.
The net gain of one is not a uniform recovery improvement. None is relabeled.

### The overlapping panels do not show a consistent gain

| Calculation | Old-control capacity /24 | New-audit capacity /39 | Old-control orders /480 | New-audit orders /390 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Original mean meaning | 24 | 37 | 434 | 272 |
| Meaning alone through connections | 24 | 36 | 439 | 271 |
| Title + meaning through connections | 23 | 37 | 434 | 271 |
| Simpler combination without connections | 23 | 37 | 432 | 276 |

The expanded ordering gain includes cross-panel comparisons; it is not a
consistent improvement within both panels. The panels overlap and are
development evidence, not independent test populations. This result does not
justify activation or a claim to have solved automatic admission.

### Verification and disposition

All six existing `test_fixed_reference_query` production-path tests pass.
No production function, prompt, hierarchy generator or test seam is changed.
All 72,450 original mean-title/meaning group scores replay exactly, along with
the preceding capacity and pair summaries. Independent dense arithmetic checks
108,675 graph-score cells, maximum error `5.55e-17`; scalar weighted-pair sums
check 9,300 labeled scores and 211,482 edge terms, maximum error `2.78e-17`.
View swaps, reversed/duplicated query order and isolated queries preserve
results. Accepted graph bytes and protected sources, labels and meanings stay
unchanged. All verification assertions pass.

Evaluation takes 2.07 seconds, reusing the fixed query/reference matrices and
small within-group accepted graphs. No outsider/outside matrix is built.
There are zero LLM calls, new encodings, training, clustering, hierarchy/UI
changes or admission-policy changes. Retain the full group/root scores,
reference edges, eight focal comparisons, per-group capacity calculations,
changed pair identities and compact `review.md` under the ignored artifact
directory. Maintained findings land on local `main`; preserve the worktree
for those frozen source and evidence paths. Both combined scores remain
experimental and unadopted.

## HR36 transfer check: source readiness and limited saved-vector control

The user requested using the second 1,000-note corpus, HR36, to test whether
the promising meaning-based approach generalizes, accepting practical
imperfection rather than requiring every development case to pass. This
section records preparation and a limited saved-vector calculation, **not a
completed transfer evaluation or an adopted admission policy**.

First disambiguate the preceding table. Its `57/63` is a label-dependent
capacity bound for arbitrary group-specific cutoffs; its `0/8` counts correctly
ordered difficult positive/negative pairs, **not zero unwanted admissions**.
Neither is an automatic rule's accuracy. Joining the existing weakest-member
rule's saved predictions to the expanded provisional Arts assessments gives
**54/63 plausible entries selected and 10/30 suspect entries selected**. These
are development cases with provisional agent judgments, not human gold or
independent validation. The new-audit cohort was chosen from that rule's
admissions, so its selection rate is not representative either.

### Independent posts and compatible sources

HR36's completed state is
`/home/dev/src/notedeck_worktrees/algos/oracle-mvp-hierarchy-hr36-v53-auto/target/hierarchy-hr36-v53-auto-thousand-note-v1/HR36/state.json`,
SHA256 `94a7f7d6d2e4ad8fac895c0c44ef4d444bece47fff60a4cbd30aaa3205a70a07`.
The native run record establishes ASv6.100 and Bootstrap-v133. Its SSE checkpoint,
base artifact, 768 dimensions, title-plus-two-newlines-plus-meaning input format
and contextual pooling implementation match the HR35 research sources.

There are **zero shared note IDs** between HR35 and HR36. HR36 has 602 authors,
181 also present in HR35: fresh posts do not establish author-independent
sampling. HR36 is automatically sampled, unlike the earlier corpus; root
composition also differs. Its six roots are Beings and society; Experience and
expression; Society and security; two separate Activities and events roots;
and Artificial intelligence. Their names alone do not define admission truth.

The completed graph represents **683 notes recursively beneath roots**; 317
are omitted. Of those 317, 254 have usable meanings, supplying **1,121 omitted
entry queries**. The other 63 have no usable Bootstrap meaning and cannot be
recovered by a meaning-vector admission rule. Count recursive descendants:
using roots' own supports alone would incorrectly report 356 omitted notes.

### Missing representation and bounded encoding requirement

The retained native HR36 worker requests/receipts select **title tokens only**,
with the meaning in context. They do not contain full-meaning pooled vectors.
Across all 5,336 usable original entries, 98 entries from 92 notes match exact
title-plus-meaning contexts in the existing HR35 numerical store. They represent
only 61 distinct contexts and mostly repeat broad people/place names.

A complete proposed check need not encode every HR36 context: the six roots'
initial accepted references plus the 1,121 eligible omitted queries require
2,511 entries / 2,502 distinct contexts. Thirty-three contexts have exact saved
meaning vectors; **2,469 require local frozen SSE encoding**. Existing root
amendments must not silently enlarge the initial reference sets. Independent
queries must never enter the accepted fusion graphs. No new LLM calls,
training or paid compute are needed for the proposed numerical check.

At this preparation checkpoint, local meaning encoding had been requested
through an asynchronous scope question. **It had not run, and no approval was
inferred from elapsed time.** The subsequent authorized test is recorded below. No HR36
scores, assessments or parameter search have been used to choose a new rule.
The proposed comparison retains the existing mean-meaning score and automatic
weakest-member boundary, with the preceding reference-only bend as an unchanged
control. Both the old Food/Arts references on new queries and HR36's own
reference groups are useful controls; neither conditional capacity nor exact
context replay substitutes for that full automatic-rule transfer test.

`42f6ef48a85687a170591be1a3f5ba9b0c55c76d3bb1f886cd966c33debb7a3e`
